# Thermal Engineering: question papers in full

44 unique papers · 2011-12 to 2022-23 · Mechanical

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

1. Solve any two from A, B, C and any two from D, E and F.

A. What is volumetric efficiency and F.A.D.? Why F.A_D. is less than swept volume? QB)

B. With the help of P-V and T-S diagrams, show that for the maximum pressure and temperature of
the cycle and the same heat rejection, Diesel cycle is more efficient than Otto cycle. @)

C. What do you mean by critical pressure ratio of a nozzle? Calculate the critical pressure ratio for
superheated steam. ; (3)

D. Explain “Mean temperature of heat addition” in context with Rankine cycle with a sketch? (4

E. What do you mean by compounding of steam turbines? Enlist its different methods. (4)

F. Draw the P-V and T-s diagram for Brayton cycle and explain all the processes associated in it. (4)

2. A. A two stage single acting reciprocating compressor takes in air at the rate of 0.2 m?/s. The intake .
al pressure and temperature of air are 0.1 MPa and 16°C. The air is compressed to a final pressure of 0.7
MPa. The intermediate pressure is ideal and intercooling is perfect. The compression index in both

<7 the stages is 1.25 and the compressor runs at 600 rpm. Neglecting clearance, Determine

a. The intermediate pressure,
b. The total volume of each cylinder,

c. The power required to drive the compressor, and

d. The rate of heat rejection in the intercooler. (7)
--- page 2 ---
B. What are rotary compressors? When the rotary compressors are used? Explain the operation of
4 centrifugal compressors with the help of velocity diagram. (7)
3. A. Ina steam nozzle, the steam expands from 4 bar to 1 bar. The initial velocity is 60 m/s and the
initial temperature is 200°C. Determine the exit velocity if the nozzle efficiency is 92%. (7)
B. Explain the term Process heat and By-product power. In a Rankine cycle, the steam at inlet to
turbine is saturated at a pressure of 35 bar and the exhaust pressure is 0.2 bar. Determine: (i) the pump
work, (ii) the turbine work, (iii) Rankine efficiency and (iv) The condenser heat flow. Assume flow
rate of 9.5 kg/s. (7)
4. A. Anair standard dual cycle has a compression ratio of 16, and compression begins at 1 bar, 50°C.
The maximum pressure is 70 bar. The heat transferred to air at constant pressure is equal to that at
constant volume. Estimate (i) the pressure and temperatures of air at the cardinal points of the cycle,
(ii) the cycle efficiency and (iii) mean effective pressure of the cycle. Assume Cy of air as 1005 J/kg-
K and Cy as 718 J/kg-K. (7)
B. In a gas turbine plant, the air is compressed in a single stage compressor from 1 bar to 9 bar and
from an initial temperature of 300K. The same air is then heated to a temperature of 800 K and then
expanded in turbine. The air is then reheated to a temperature of 800 K and then expanded in second
turbine. Find the maximum power that can be obtained from the installation, if the mass of air
circulated per second is 2 kg. Assume Cp of air as 1 kJ/kg-K (7)
5. _ A. Air enters a frictionless adiabatic converging nozzle at 10 bar and 500 K with negligible velocity.
The nozzle discharges to a region at 2 bar. If the exit area of the nozzle is 2.5 cm”, find the flow rate
of air through the nozzle. Assume Cp of air as 1005 J/kg-K and Cy as 718 J/kg-K. (7)
B. A steam power plant operates on theoretical reheat cycle. Steam at boiler at 150 bar, 500°C
expands through the high pressure turbine. It is reheated at a constant pressure of 40 bar to 500°C and
expands through the low pressure turbine to a condenser at 0.1 bar. Draw the T-s and h-s diagrams
and find the quality of steam at turbine exhaust and cycle efficiency. (7)
6. A. Explain the functions of the blading of a reaction turbine. A certain stage of a parson’s turbine
consists of one row of fixed blades and one row of moving blades. Calculate the power developed in
the stage if the mean diameter of the blades is 68 cm, rpm of turbine is 3000, mass of steam passing
per second as 13.5 kg, steam velocity at exit from exit blades is 143.7 m/s and the blade outlet angle
is 20°. (8)
B. The velocity of steam exiting the nozzle of the impulse stage of a turbine is 400 m/s. the blades
operate close to the maximum blading efficiency. The nozzle angle is 20°. Considering equiangular

blades and neglecting blade friction, calculate the diagram power and the diagram efficiency for a

steam flow of 0.6 kg/s. (6)

. (a) Ina single-stage, single-acting reciprocating air compressor running at 200 rpm, air is

mass-flow rate of air is 200 kg/h.Take R= 0.287 kJ/kg

statement with necessary diagrams.

temperature.

an enthalpy of 2935 kJ/kg to an enthalpy of 2584 kJ/kg at 0.1 bar. The nozzle makes

blade velocity that produces maximum efficiency for a turbine speed of 3600 rpm.

Assume that steam enters the nozzle with negligible velocity.

I   heat is lost to the surroundings which is at 98 kPa, 20 OC. Neglecting the K.E. and P.E. changes,
determine per kg of air (i) the decrease in availability, (ii) the maximum work, and (iii) the irreversibility.
For air, take c, = 1.005 kJ/kgK and h = c,T . T is in Kelvin.   14M
3. (a) Make a comparison of Rankine and Carnot cycles. Explain why Carnot cycle is not practicable.   8M
(b) In a single-heater regenerative cycle the steam enters the turbine with an enthalpy 3230.9 kJ1kg and
leaves the turbine with an enthalpy 2192.2 kJkg. The feed water heating is done by steam bled at 5
bar, enthalpy 2796 kJlkg. The feed water heater is a direct contact type which operates at 5 bar. The
condensate from a condenser with an enthalpy of 191.83 kJlkg enters into the feed heater. The water
leaving the feed heater is saturated liquid at 5 bar (enthalpy 640.23 kJlkg ). Neglect pump work.
Determine (i) the cycle efficiency and the steam rate (kg/kW . -   h) and (ii) the increase in efficiency and
steam rate, as compared to the Rankine cycle (without regeneration).   12M
4?(a) Explain the effect of intercooling and reheating on gas turbine cycle (Joule cycle ).   6M
(b) In a gas turbine plant the ratio of T,,,   / Tmin is fixed. Arrangement consists of a single-stage
compression followed by expansion in two turbines of equal pressure ratios with reheat to the maximum
cycle temperature. If qc and r   l are ~   the compressor and turbine efficiencies (isentropic efficiencies),
(i) derive the expression for the overall pressure ratio corresponding to the optimum specific output.   If
qc is 0.85 and r   l is ~ 0.9, y is 1.4 and T,,, I T,,   is 3.5, determine (ii) the above pressure ratio for
optimum specific output and (iii) optimum specific output in terms of c, and Tmi,.   14M
--- page 2 ---
5. (a) Explain with a sketch the working of a centrifugal compressdr. Show the pressure and velocity
variations in impeller and diffuser.   6M
(b) A single-acting.twostage reciprocating air compressor with complete intercooling and minimum power
requirement delivers 10.5 kglmin of air at 16 bar. The suction occurs at 1 bar and 27 OC. The
compression and expansion processes are reversible, polytropic index n = 1.3. Calculate (i) the power
required to drive the compressor (ii) the isothermal efficiency (iii) the free air delivery (iv) the heat
transferred in intercooler. R = 0.287 kJkgK and c,, = 1.005 kJ/kgK.
The compressor runs at 440 RPM. (v) If the clearance ratios for L.P. and H.P. cylinders are 0.04 and
0.06 respectively, calculate the swept and clearance volumes for each cylinder.   14M
6. (a) Explain (i) the velocity compounded impulse steam turbine and (ii) pressure compounded impulse
steam turbine showing pressure and velocity variations along the axis of the turbine.   6M
(b) A simple impulse turbine has a mean blade speed of 200 mls. The nozzles are inclined at 20° to the
plane of rotation of the blades. The steam velocity from the nozzles is 600 mls. The turbine uses 3500
kglh of steam. The absolute velocity at exit is along the axis of t h turbine. ~   Assuming equiangular
blades (moving blade inlet and outlet angles are equal), determine (i) the inlet and outlet angles of the
blades, (ii) the power output of the turbine, (iii) the diagram efficiency and (iv) the axial thrust.   14M
7. (a) Explain critical pressure ratio and maximum discharge through nozzle.   6M
(b) Determine the throat area, exit area and exit velocity for a steam nozzle to pass a mass flow of 0.2 kgls
when inlet conditions are 10 bar and 250 OC and the final pressure is 2 bar. Assume expansion is
isentropic and that the inlet velocity is negligible. Use P V ' . ~ = constant and ideal gas equation of state
with R = 0.462 kJ1kgK.   14M

3. Answer to each new question to be started on fresh page.
4. Figures in brackets on the right hand side indicate full marks.
} 5. Assume suitable data if required but state and justify the same.

6. Use of Steam Table, Mollier Diagram, and Gas Tables is allowed.

Q1)a)i) A high pressure boiler delivers steam at 90 bar and 480°C. The steam is expanded in the
first section of the steam turbine to 12 bar and then withdrawn and passed on to the reheater,
where it is reheated to initial temperature. The expansion now takes place in the remaining
section, of steam turbine, down to the condenser pressure of 0.07 bar. Determine the efficiency of
ideal reheat cycle for flow of 1 kg/sec of steam. _ (Marks:06)

iif Wy is Carnot cycle not practicable for a steam power plant? (Marks:04)

QI)b)i) An ideal regenerative steam turbine is admitted with 68,000 kg/hr of steam. It has one
heater to which 15,420 kg/hr of steam is bled at 10 bar, the remaining being received by
condenser at 0.07 bar. Determine the state of steam: 1.at the heater entrance and 2.at the
condenser entrance. (Marks:06)

ii) What is a binary vapour cycle? (Marks:04)

Ve
--- page 2 ---
Q2)a) A gas turbine unit has a pressure ratio of 6:1 and maximum cycle temperature 610°C. The
isentropic efficiencies of the compressor and turbine are 0.80 and 0.82 respectively. Calculate the
power output in kilowatts of an electric generator geared to the turbine, When air enters the
compressor at 15°C at the rate of 16 kg/sec. (Take ep= 1.005 kd/kg-K, y = 1.4 for the
compression process and Take ¢,= 1.11 kJ/kg-K, y = 1.333 for the expansion process)

(Marks: 10)
Q2)b)i) What is the effect of regeneration on Brayton cycle efficiency? (Marks:04)

ii) In a gas turbine plant, working on Brayton cycle with regenerator of 76% effectiveness,
the air at the inlet to the compressor is at 0.1 MPa, 30°C, the pressure ratio is 6 and the maximum
cycle temperature is 900°C. If the turbine and compressor have each an efficiency of 80%, find
the increase in the cycle efficiency due to regeneration. (Marks:06)

Q3)a) An air compressor having stroke length of 88 cm and clearance volume of 2% of the swept
volume deliver air at a pressure of 8.2 bar. In order to study the effect of clearance on free air
delivery and work expended, the compressor was overhauled and a ‘distance piece’ of 0.55 cm
was fitted between the cylinder head and cylinder. The compressor was then commissioned
under the changed clearance. Calculate: 1,Percentage change in the volume of free air delivered
and 2.Percentage change in power expended.

Before and after overhauling the piston had a suction pressure 1.025 bar and index of
compression and expansion was 1,3 (Marks:10)

}Q3)b)i) Explain the advantages and disadvantages of multistage compression. (Marks:05)

ii) A two stage air compressor with complete intercooling delivers air to the mains at a
pressure of 30 bar, the suction condition being 1 bar and 15°C. If both cylinders have the same
stroke, find the ratio of cylinders diameters for the efficiency of compression to be a maximum,
Assume the index of compression in both cylinders to be 1.3 (Marks:05)

Q4)a) A convergent-divergent nozzle has a throat area 500 mm” and exit area 1000 mm”. Air
enters the nozzle with a stagnation temperature of 360 K and stagnation pressure of 1 MPa.
Determine the maximum flow rate of air that the nozzle can pass and the static pressure, static
temperature, Mach Number and velocity at the exit from nozzle, if 1.the divergent section acts as
a nozzle and 2.the divergent section acts as a diffuser: (Marks:10)

Q4)b)i) Dry saturated steam at pressure of 6 bar flows through nozzle at the rate of 4.5 kg/sec
and discharges at a pressure of 1.6 bar. The loss due to friction occurs only in the diverging
portion of the nozzle and its magnitude is 12% of the total isentropic enthalpy drop. Assume the
isentropic index of expansion n = 1.135, determine the cross-sectional area at the throat and the
exit of the nozzle. (Marks:06)

2h
--- page 3 ---
Q4)b) ii) What do you understand by choking in nozzle flows? (Marks:04)

Q5)a) A single stage impulse turbine rotor has a diameter of 1.2m running at 3000 r.p.m. The
nozzle angle is 18°. Blade speed ratio is 0.42. The ratio of the relative velocity at outlet to the
relative velocity at inlet is 0.9. The outlet angle of the blade is 3° smaller than the inlet angle. The
steam flow rate is 5 kg/sec. Draw the velocity diagram and find following:

1.Velocity of whirl 2.Axial thrust on bearing 3.Blade angles 4. Power developed. (Marks:10)

Q5)b) Derive the expression for the maximum blade efficiency in a single stage reaction turbine.
(Marks: 10)
Q6) a)i) What is the available energy referred to a cycle? (Marks:05)
ii) What is mean by Quality of energy? (Marks:05)

Q6)b) i) Calculate the available energy in 40 kg of water at 75°C with respect to the surroundings
at 5°C, the pressure of water being | atm. (Marks:05)

ii) Give Comparison between Reciprocating and Rotary air compressors. (Marks:05)

Q7) Write short note (any four): (Marks:20)
1. Velocity Compounding in steam turbines.

2. Effect of clearance volume in air compressors.

3. Fanno Lines

4. Availability in Steady Flow Processes

5. Gouy-Stodola Theorem

6. Mach Number

7. Use of Gas Tables

8.'¢Governing of Steam Turbines
